The collective contract of employment is organised by law." It has been expressed as being the basis and procedure of the use of the right by Group Business Convention 2822, Strike and the Lokaoutt Law, which is still in effect in this framework. As of 31/1/2012, there has been a serious problem with the use of the right and freedom established in our Constitution, causing workers to be unable to use collective bargaining rights. Trade unions seeking collective employment agreements have been left unresponsive by the Ministry of Labor and Social Security, with no response in favor or against them. In an official statement from 1/2/2012 to 8/5/2012, Mr. Minister's official statement has reported that there are 631 applications waiting for authorization for a collective contract of employment. However, during the talks, the minister stated that the number exceeds 900, and there are more than 200,000 workers within the scope of these applications. First of all, the right to a collective contract is a right set up in Article 53 of our Constitution, and there is a situation in which the rights are blocked. In addition, the third article in Article 12 of Article No. 2822, Article 2822, which is currently in effect, reads: "The ten per cent of workers working in a working company is based on statistics published by the Ministry of Labor and Social Security each year in January and July. The number of workers in this business is valid until the number of members of the trade unions in this business is published for collective contract and other transactions. Statistics that will be issued later will not affect the authority of the labour union, which has applied for or obtained a warrant to obtain a warrant. The ministry is based on membership and resignation notifications sent to it from 1/8/2010 and employee notifications to the Social Security Agency in the determination of the official union and organisation of statistics. The latest employee and member statistics, published by the Ministry by this date, are valid." The verdict is in. According to this verdict, the procedure must be carried out according to the statistics published by the Ministry at the latest. The latest statistics published by the ministry is the statistics published on its website and in the Official Gazette in July 2009. Thus, the nullification of the minister's reply is against the law. In addition, the second article of Article 13 of the relevant law is required to answer the union's application for authority within six work days. The ministry also acts against this obligation. tbmm-2014-06-10sit03spe0121par0219	Mr. President, dear lawmakers, at the beginning of my words, I salute you all. We're negotiating a bill of law that contains very important changes. Many issues have been discussed, and then, of course, it will be discussed, but the most important thing of all is the opening of the legal judiciary. Of course, the main thing is that the judicial judiciary will be brought into action in the course of our law in the coming time. On the contrary, it is not the first-degree court's decision, but the first-degree court's own discrepancy that has been resolved. Therefore, court of law is considered second-degree courts in its doctrine, since it is as competent as the courts that first discussed conflict. The litigation courts allow new evidence to be applied as they can reassess evidence collected in their first-degree courts. In this case, the court of law may be able to listen to witnesses and explore by opening a trial. The principles that dominate the first-degree courts, such as openness, verbality, face - to - face, are the same in the context of the main principles of the court. When we look at the functioning of the litigation courts, the courts will reject the appeal if they find the first-degree court's decision on the matter they're looking at is in accordance with the law. This rejection will mean that the first-degree court decision will be approved. If he comes to the conclusion that there are specific reasons for violation of the law in the first-degree court's decision, he will break it and send it to the first-degree court. If he finds the first-degree court's decision against the law, he'll accept the application for a recrimination and dismiss the local court's decision and decide on the basis of the matter by retrialing as if it were his first-degree trial. In the criminal department of the judicial court, the first-level courts will issue prison sentences over five years, and in the legal department, cases that exceed the value of 25,000 Turkish lira and some legal cases that cannot be assessed with money will be seen in the Supreme Court. With this arrangement, there is also a path of disputation in the administrative judiciary. There is a separate explanation in the law of those who will be examined in the Court of Appeal and those who will be finalised in the indictment. Ladies and gentlemen of the law, the indictment is essentially not a foreign institution to our law. From 1879 to 1924, it was implemented, which was abolished in 1924. In Turkey, many lawyers, scientists who have studied the Supreme Court and law schools have expressed that it is a necessity for Turkey and that there is a great flaw in its not being established. In 1932, 1963, 1977, 1978 and 1993, we see drafting draft laws on the re-establishment of law courts, but they have not been implemented. During the time of our governments, the draft law on the establishment of district court courts has been passed, and we are now taking steps to establish the establishment of regional administrative courts. Today, in many countries such as Germany, France, Britain, Italy, the Netherlands, Spain, Sweden, Austria, Greece, Hungary, Serbia, there are law-enforcement courts and activities around here. I'd like to make a few things clear. Some speakers perceived the reference as a sign that Turkey would be divided into states, and they made assessments in this regard. If that's the way we take it, the district administration courts have been in force for decades. Did Turkey split up when there were district administration courts? It's not divided. It's extremely wrong to make such an assessment from here. I'd like to underline that in particular. I can't agree with the assessments that opening the law will prolong the trials, prevent the citizens from getting their dues on time, cause a lot of problems. When we look at it, the Supreme Court has 390,000 files in the criminal offices today, 505,000 in the legal offices. These are awaiting an appeal. After the execution of these regulations, 90 per cent of cases will be finalized in the sentence, while 89 per cent of cases in the law will be finalised in the indictment. Therefore the number of cases that have been established in the indictment will increase, and the number of cases that go to the Supreme Court and the number of cases that go to the Court will be reduced. What does that mean? One: the Supreme Court will decide faster, and will be able to review the files in front of it and link it to the decision. He will also make a swifter decision, and he will see to it that many lacks are treated as if they were first-degree courts, and he will evaluate them, and he will help our citizen get his due earlier. This is an important legal institution that shortens the judicial process, not prolongs the judicial process, but helps justice in its time. You have to read this right, but at this point, unfortunately, the evaluations are quite different. tbmm-2014-06-10sit03spe0127par0245	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on my own behalf on the second chapter of the draft law on the amendment of Turkish Penal Code to some Laws. I salute you all. The law is a bill designed to speed up judicial services, effectively and efficiently, and to increase the rate of punishment for some crimes that have hurt the public conscience. We can list regulations under criminal law, administrative judiciary, amendments to the Supreme Court's Law and other regulations. The changes made in the field of criminal law are as follows: In connection with the crimes committed against sexual immunity, the amendments made in Articles 102, 103, 104 and 105 of the Turkish Penal Code are significantly increased. In the event that the crime is committed against children, those sentenced to aggravated life imprisonment will remain in prison for 33 years instead of 30 years, and those sentenced to life imprisonment for 24 years instead of 33 years. If the crime remains on the level of harassment, if the victim is an adult, he will be sentenced from 2 to 5 years, while the victim will be sentenced to 3 to 8 years in prison. Peace is being handed over to the criminal courts to judge crimes that have entered the jurisdiction of the criminal courts in accordance with the regulation of the lift of the criminal distinction, as well as to the establishment of judicial justices to make decisions that should be made by the judge in the investigation process, thus providing for a more effective security of the basic rights and freedoms, and bringing judicial activities to a fairer place. In the regulation on drugs and stimulants, the sentence for cannabis planting is increased from 1 to 7 years in prison, from 4 to 12 years in prison, while drugs and stimulants are sentenced to 10 to 20 years in prison for exporting and importing drugs. The penalty for selling drugs and stimulants is from 5 to 15 years in prison from 10 to 20 years. If drugs and stimulants are given to the child or sold, the prison sentence will not be less than 15 years. The charge of theft is suspended from 5 to 10 years in prison from 2 to 5 years in prison. The punishment for the theft at night can be increased by up to one third, and then half of it will be increased. In crimes of communication, energy, railway and airway transportation, theft and harming property are often committed together in crimes of theft and harming property. As a result of theft and harming property, the punishment for public service related to communication, energy, railway and airway will be doubled, even if it is temporary. The penal institution is required not to pose a danger to public safety in order to delay the execution of prisoners who are unable to survive alone due to illness or disability. The concept is consolidated and consolidated by adding the criteria and the concreteity of this element of danger. As for administrative provisions: As everyone knows, the period of finalisation of files coming to the State has been extended due to heavy workloads. The introduction of the law of the exception is also a matter that has been discussed since long ago. The bill will be restructured by the amendment to the Law on Adjudication as a recrimination of the district administrative courts, and it will decide on the application of adjudications against the decisions of administrative and tax courts. The number of regional administrative courts will be reduced according to the new system, and there will be a number of regional administrative courts that will be determined according to the need. Only decisions that will be made after the examination of the exception can be applied to the appeals of those mentioned in Article 46 of Law 2577. Under Article 2577 of Law 45, the decisions made against tax cases, full judicial cases, and evaluations against administrative proceedings will be final, and will not be applied to the basis of reference and appeal for these decisions. Finally, the administrative judiciary has a new method of swift judgment. With all these arrangements, suffering and victims from the delay will be eliminated. I wish the bill to be good luck to our nation and to the judicial community, and I salute our beloved nation and your delegation again.
